TL;DR: 评估对比激活加法(CAA)能否在不更新模型权重的情况下，提升医学视觉-语言模型对肺炎分类的诊断性能，结果显示在三款胸片VLM中，其中一款模型获得了有意义的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理时间工程可以改变模型行为而无需微调，但这种方法在医学视觉-语言模型中提升诊断性能的实用性尚不明确。本研究旨在探究CAA能否在不更新模型权重的情况下改善胸片VLM的肺炎分类性能。

Method: 使用三款冻结的胸片VLM(MedGemma-4B-IT、NV-Reason-CXR-3B和CheXOne-3B)在公开的Kermany肺炎测试集上进行评估。分类基于二元提示下"Yes"和"No"token的对数概率。引入了三种引导向量：30对答案偏置控制、30对肺炎文本对比，以及基于30张肺炎和30张正常开发图像生成的图像条件对比。使用200张确定性开发集进行层和尺度选择(100张)以及阈值校准(100张)。通过ROC-AUC、PR-AUC、F1分数、阈值分析、反向向量控制、随机向量控制和条件自举置信区间等多种指标评估性能。

Result: 固定阈值F1改善经常出现，但并不总是表明诊断性能的真正提升。对于NV-Reason-CXR-3B，校准后F1从零样本设置的0.7692提升至肺炎文本引导的0.8619和图像条件引导的0.8727。对于CheXOne-3B，肺炎文本引导使校准F1从0.8528提升至0.8666，但置信区间跨越零。MedGemma-4B-IT未见一致改善。总体上，CAA显著改变了预测分数分布和操作特性，无需微调即可实现。

Conclusion: 在公开肺炎基准测试中，CAA在不进行微调的情况下显著改变了预测分数分布和操作特性。在三款评估的VLM中，有一款观察到了有意义的性能提升，表明激活引导可能作为调整医学VLM行为的轻量级方法。

Abstract: Inference-time engineering can alter model behavior without fine-tuning. However, its utility for improving diagnostic performance in medical vision-language models (VLMs) remains unclear. We aim to evaluate whether Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) can improve pneumonia classification in chest radiograph VLMs without updating model weights. Three frozen chest radiograph VLMs (MedGemma-4B-IT, NV-Reason-CXR-3B, and CheXOne-3B) were evaluated on the public Kermany pneumonia test set. Classification was based on the logits of the tokens Yes and No under a binary prompt. Steering vectors included a 30-pair answer-bias control, a 30-pair pneumonia text contrast, and an image-conditioned contrast derived from 30 pneumonia and 30 normal development images. A deterministic 200-image development set was used for layer and scale selection (100 images) and threshold calibration (100 images). Performance was assessed using ROC-AUC, PR-AUC, F1 score, threshold analyses, reverse-vector controls, random-vector controls, and conditional bootstrap confidence intervals. Fixed-threshold F1 improvements were frequently observed but did not consistently indicate improved diagnostic performance. For MedGemma-4B-IT. NV-Reason-CXR-3B showed the strongest benefit: calibrated F1 improved from 0.7692 in the zero-shot setting to 0.8619 with pneumonia-text steering and to 0.8727 with image-conditioned steering. For CheXOne-3B, pneumonia-text steering increased calibrated F1 from 0.8528 to 0.8666, although the confidence interval crossed zero. On this public pneumonia benchmark, CAA substantially altered prediction score distributions and operating characteristics without fine-tuning. Meaningful performance gains were observed in one of three evaluated VLMs, suggesting that activation steering may serve as a lightweight approach for adapting medical VLM behavior.

</details>


### [36] [Stochastic Signed Distance Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20856)
*Hiroki Sakuma,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSDP方法，将SDF体渲染重新表述为概率表面渲染，通过建模光线与表面的随机首次相遇时间分布，实现了更好的表面重建和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角表面重建是计算机视觉的核心问题。现有基于符号距离场(SDF)的方法通常使用体渲染来处理可见性问题，但这些方法缺乏对不确定性的显式建模。本文旨在提供更严格的概率框架来改进表面重建质量。

Method: 提出随机符号距离过程(SSDP)，将每条光线上的SDF建模为随机过程，从而诱导出光线与表面首次相遇时间的分布。基于贝叶斯滤波推导每个采样区间的首次通过概率，并提供适合并行渲染的实用近似。该方法包含现有NeuS方法作为特例。

Result: 在DTU和MobileBrick数据集上的实验表明，该方法在表面重建质量上优于基线方法，同时在不确定性量化方面也表现更好，验证了首次通过公式的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将SDF体重新表述为概率表面渲染的SSDP框架，不仅提供了更严格的数学基础，而且在重建精度和不确定性建模方面都取得了改进，为多视角表面重建提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Multi-view surface reconstruction is a core problem in computer vision. One prominent line of work represents the surface implicitly as a signed distance field (SDF), optimizing it based on the photometric loss between rendered and observed pixel colors. These approaches typically employ SDF-based volume rendering to obtain a differentiable relaxation of discontinuous visibility along rays, thereby reducing reliance on silhouette supervision. In this paper, we reformulate SDF-based volume rendering as probabilistic surface rendering, where each pixel color is modeled as a mixture distribution induced by the random first ray-surface intersection. To this end, we introduce Stochastic Signed Distance Processes (SSDP), which model the SDF along each ray as a stochastic process, inducing a first-passage-time distribution for each ray. We then derive the first-passage probability for each sampling interval based on Bayesian filtering, together with its practical approximation for parallel rendering. We further show that NeuS, an existing SDF-based volume rendering method, arises as a special case of our formulation. Experiments on the DTU and MobileBrick datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines in both surface reconstruction and uncertainty quantification, supporting the effectiveness of our first-passage formulation. Our code is available at https://github.com/skmhrk1209/SSDP.

</details>


### [37] [FOCA: Future-Oriented Conditioning for Data-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20867)
*Duc Minh Nguyen,Nghiem Tuong Diep,Binh Gia Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Doanh Le,Tan Q. Nguyen,Thien-Loc Ha,Nhiem Tran,Bao Thach,Nhat X. Tran,Tuan A. Tran,Artur Habuda,Philip Lund Møller,Tran Nguyen Le,Daniel Sonntag,Matthias Niepert,Khoa D. Doan,Vu Duong,Hung Ngo,Minh N. Vu,Duy M. H. Nguyen,An Thai Le,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOCA是一个面向未来的条件化框架，用于数据高效的视觉-语言-动作模型适配，通过预测未来交互嵌入和与未来目标观察对齐，显著提升了小样本模仿学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型在小样本模仿学习中表现不佳，当演示数据减少时性能急剧下降，揭示了当前适配策略的关键弱点。

Method: FOCA框架结合了显式预测任务相关的未来交互嵌入和隐式对齐未来目标观察，支持在潜在空间进行长程推理而无需像素级预测。该方法还支持与视频世界模型生成的合成视频进行无动作共同训练。

Result: 在LIBERO数据集上使用20个演示达到95.7%成功率，在RoboCasa上提升7-12%，在真实机器人上实现高达26%的绝对性能提升，建立了小样本VLA适配的新技术水平。

Conclusion: FOCA通过未来导向的条件化机制有效解决了小样本VLA适配的挑战，实现了数据高效的学习，可解释为学习未来条件的价值类表示，为机器人控制中的小样本学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic control via large-scale multimodal pretraining, yet their effectiveness under few-shot imitation learning remains limited. We conduct a systematic stress test of state-of-the-art VLA models and show that performance degrades sharply as demonstrations are reduced, revealing a key weakness of existing adaptation strategies. To address this, we introduce FOCA, a future-oriented conditioning framework for data-efficient VLA adaptation. FOCA combines explicit prediction of task-grounded future interaction embeddings with implicit alignment to future goal observations, enabling long-horizon reasoning in latent space without pixel-level prediction. This formulation naturally supports action-free co-training with synthetic videos from video world models and can be interpreted as learning a future-conditioned value-like representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate FOCA achieves 95.7% success with 20 demonstrations on LIBERO, improves 7-12% on RoboCasa, and delivers up to 26% absolute gains on real robots, establishing a new state of the art in few-shot VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [38] [Toward Parking Spot Occupancy Recognition: A Self-Supervised Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20886)
*Luan Marko Kujavski,Rayson Laroca,Paulo Lisboa de Almeida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督的停车位占用识别方法，无需目标停车场的标注样本，通过两阶段自监督训练和一次性监督微调实现高性能，并引入了两阶段部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着城市扩张，自动监控停车场对高效可持续城市至关重要。传统方法需要大量标注数据，成本高昂且不适用于新停车场。需要一种无需目标停车场标注样本的解决方案。

Method: 采用基于SimCLR的自监督迁移学习微调协议：第一阶段在无标签通用数据上进行自监督预训练，第二阶段在无标签目标特定数据上进行自监督训练，最后使用通用停车场标签进行一次性监督微调。采用ResNet-50编码器，在PKLot、CNRPark-EXT和PLds三个公开数据集上通过留一交叉环境协议评估。

Result: 通用强模型单独表现优于监督和自监督基线，平均准确率达97.2%；采用提出的两阶段策略后提升至97.8%。两阶段部署策略中，首先部署通用强模型，然后利用前N天收集的无标签图像通过自监督训练生成专用模型。

Conclusion: 自监督学习为真实世界停车占用监控提供了可扩展和标签高效的解决方案，模型和源代码已公开。

Abstract: As urban areas expand, automatic monitoring of parking lots becomes essential for efficient and sustainable cities. This work proposes a self-supervised approach for parking spot occupancy recognition that requires no labeled samples from the target parking lot. Building upon a self-supervised transfer learning fine-tuning protocol, the proposed training strategy consists of two self-supervised stages: first on unlabeled generic data and then on unlabeled target-specific data, followed by supervised fine-tuning using only generic parking lot labels. We adopt SimCLR with a ResNet-50 encoder and evaluate the method under a leave-one-out cross-environment protocol on three public datasets: PKLot, CNRPark-EXT, and PLds. We also introduce a two-stage deployment strategy in which a Strong General Model is initially deployed, followed by a Specialized Model that incorporates unlabeled images collected during the first N days of deployment in a self-supervised manner. Experimental results show that the Strong General Model alone outperforms supervised and self-supervised baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 97.2%, which further improves to 97.8% with the proposed two-stage strategy. These results demonstrate that self-supervised learning enables a scalable and labelefficient solution for real-world parking occupancy monitoring. Our trained models and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/LoanMaikon/Parking-Spot-Occupancy-Recognition.

</details>


### [39] [Fine-grained Human Motion Understanding with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20888)
*Thomas Markhorst,Zhi-Yi Lin,Jouh Yeong Chew,Jan van Gemert,Xucong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的细粒度人体运动理解模型，通过带有时间戳的骨骼姿态序列表示运动，支持2D和3D骨骼表示，并在多种运动语言推理任务上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体运动理解方法通常依赖于3D运动捕捉数据，缺乏对2D骨骼表示的支持，并且在运动的时间信息（顺序、持续时间和节奏）建模方面存在不足，限制了细粒度运动理解的能力。

Method: 方法名为"methodname"，将运动表示为带有明确时间戳的骨骼姿态序列，每个姿态编码身体关节位置。采用统一的姿态编码器支持2D和3D骨骼表示，可选择性结合视频上下文信息。通过多样化的训练混合（姿态标注、姿态问答、运动标注、运动问答）来研究运动-语言推理所需的监督方式。

Result: 在BABEL-QA、HuMMan-QA、CompMo、NTU-RGB+D和QEVD-Coach等多个基准测试上实现了最先进的性能。消融研究表明，主要收益来自姿态和运动级监督的多样性，而分阶段训练提供较小的额外收益。即使仅使用2D骨骼输入，该方法也超过了之前的3D方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了显式时间编码和多样化的姿态/运动级监督对于细粒度人体运动理解的有效性。提出的统一框架支持多种运动表示，为运动语言理解提供了强大的基础。

Abstract: In this work, we propose \methodname, an LLM-based model for fine-grained human motion understanding that represents motion as a sequence of skeletal poses with explicit timestamps for each pose. Each pose encodes body joint positions and is temporally grounded with timestamp tokens, allowing the model to reason about motion order, duration, and rhythm. To study what supervision is needed for motion-language reasoning, we construct a diverse training mixture spanning pose captioning, pose question answering, motion captioning, and motion question answering. Our ablations show that the primary gains come from the diversity of pose- and motion-level supervision, while staged training provides a smaller additional benefit. Different from previous works that rely on ground-truth 3D motion capture, our approach supports both 2D and 3D skeletal motion representations through a unified pose encoder, and can optionally incorporate video to provide contextual information. Extensive experiments on BABEL-QA, HuMMan-QA, CompMo, NTU-RGB+D, and QEVD-Coach demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit temporal encoding and diverse pose- and motion-level supervision for fine-grained human motion understanding. Notably, even when using only 2D skeletal input, our approach surpasses previous 3D-based methods.

</details>


### [40] [Go-with-the-Track: Video Compositing and Motion Control with Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20891)
*Koichi Namekata,Yash Kant,Zhizheng Liu,Ryan D Burgert,Yuancheng Xu,Kuan Heng Lin,Emmett Steven,Julien Philip,Li Ma,Andrea Vedaldi,Paul Debevec,Ning Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Go-with-the-Track是一种统一的视频生成框架，通过联合多参考图像和参考锚点轨迹进行条件控制，实现了精确的运动控制和图像合成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将运动控制和参考图像合成视为独立任务：基于点轨迹的图像到视频模型仅允许在第一帧插入内容，而基于参考的视频模型缺乏对参考内容在帧间时空整合的细粒度控制。需要一种统一方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 引入参考锚点轨迹，建立生成帧与参考图像间的明确对应关系；提出空间感知的点轨迹嵌入编码，通过坐标MLP和时序池化捕获轨迹空间特征；设计轻量适配器将点轨迹嵌入注入视频扩散变换器，解决像素到补丁分辨率不匹配问题；采用混合训练策略在动态、静态和合成场景视频数据集上联合训练以提升运动可控性。

Result: 实现了单一模型中优越的运动和参考控制，并支持新能力：基于多参考条件和点轨迹驱动的视频生成与合成，以及对静态和动态场景的相机控制。

Conclusion: Go-with-the-Track统一了运动控制和参考图像合成能力，通过参考锚点轨迹和空间感知点轨迹嵌入的创新设计，在单一模型中实现了精确的视频生成控制，为影视制作等领域提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Filmmaking demands precise motion control and reference image compositing -- capabilities that existing methods treat separately. Point-track-conditioned image-to-video models restrict content insertion to the first frame, while reference-to-video models lack fine-grained spatial-temporal control over how reference content integrates across frames.
  We present Go-with-the-Track, which unifies both capabilities by jointly conditioning on multiple reference images and reference-anchored point-tracks -- extending conventional point-tracks to explicitly establish correspondences between generated frames and reference images, thus enabling precise compositing and motion control throughout the video.
  To achieve this, we introduce spatially-aware point-track embeddings that encode the full sequence of point-track coordinates using a coordinate-wise MLP followed by temporal pooling. This representation captures the spatial characteristics of each point-track (serving as a unique identifier), while the embedding similarity correlates directly with spatial proximity, enhancing the model's ability to distinguish and associate point-tracks. We inject these point-track embeddings into a video diffusion transformer via a lightweight adapter, resolving the pixel-to-patch resolution mismatch while avoiding the substantial motion detail loss inherent in naive point-track subsampling.
  We use a hybrid training strategy to train jointly on dynamic, static, and synthetic scene video datasets to boost motion controllability. Experiments demonstrate that Go-with-the-Track achieves superior motion and reference control in a single model and enables new capabilities: multi-reference conditioned video generation with point-track driven compositing, as well as camera control for both static and dynamic scenes. Project Page: https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Go-with-the-Track/

</details>


### [41] [BELDE: Building a Large-scale Earth-observation Land-cover Dataset for Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20909)
*Ümit Mert Çağlar,Alptekin Temizel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BELDE是专为基于RGB的遥感语义分割设计的大规模公开数据集，覆盖欧洲并包含韩国、美国两个扩展区域，提供超过百万图像-分割图对，旨在促进遥感模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管真彩色（RGB）图像在许多卫星和航空平台中广泛应用，但现有的土地覆盖分割数据集往往在地理覆盖范围、规模或公开可访问性方面存在局限，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 基于Sentinel-2真彩色图像和ESA WorldCover数据标注，构建包含1,088,385个图像-分割图对的大型数据集，覆盖欧洲7类土地覆盖（10米空间分辨率），并创建了韩国和加利福尼亚-内华达的扩展子集。

Result: 基于BELDE训练的分割模型在欧洲测试集上达到83.0%的F1分数，但在BELDE-CA-NV和BELDE-K上分别降至66.4%和58.3%，揭示了地理分布外泛化的挑战。

Conclusion: BELDE作为大陆尺度的RGB分割评估基准，支持鲁棒且可迁移的地球观测模型开发，公开的数据集和基准资源将推动遥感研究进展。

Abstract: Earth observation imagery plays a critical role in environmental monitoring, urban planning, disaster assessment, and climate analysis. While multi-spectral sensors are increasingly available, true-color (RGB) imagery remains widely used due to the power, cost, and deployment constraints of many satellite and aerial platforms. However, existing land-cover segmentation datasets are often limited in geographic coverage, scale, or public accessibility.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce BELDE (Building a Large-scale Earth-observation Land-cover Dataset for Europe), a publicly available dataset tailored for RGB-based remote sensing semantic segmentation. Constructed from Sentinel-2 true-color images and ESA WorldCover data annotations, BELDE contains 1,088,385 curated image-segmentation map pairs spanning Europe with 7 land-cover classes at 10 m spatial resolution, making it one of the largest publicly available RGB land-cover segmentation datasets for Earth observation. To facilitate cross-region generalization studies, we additionally introduce BELDE-K (16,607 pairs) covering the Republic of Korea and BELDE-CA-NV (88,155 pairs) covering California and Nevada in the United States.
  We establish baseline results using multiple semantic segmentation architectures and evaluate both in-domain and cross-domain performance. Models trained on BELDE achieve an F1 score of 83.0% on the European test set, while performance decreases to 66.4% on BELDE-CA-NV and 58.3% on BELDE-K, highlighting the challenges posed by out-of-distribution geographic domain shift. By providing a continental-scale RGB segmentation and evaluation benchmark, BELDE supports the development of robust and transferable Earth observation models. The dataset and benchmark resources will be publicly released.

</details>


### [42] [PROTON: Prototype-Based Test-Time Online OOD Detection for Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20913)
*Abhijit Das,Nichula Wasalathilaka,Yifan Lu,Adinath Dukre,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Shadab Khan,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROTON：基于原型的测试时在线OOD检测方法，无需模型修改、训练数据或提示工程，通过在VLM嵌入空间中维护在线原型库，自适应融合原型距离与MCM评分，显著提升医疗视觉语言模型在协变量偏移、语义偏移和远分布偏移上的OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言模型（VLMs）在零样本临床图像分类中，缺乏可靠的部署时分布外（OOD）检测方法。静态评分方法无法在所有偏移类型中一致有效：例如MCM方法在远分布偏移上表现良好（AUROC 76.4%），但在协变量偏移（如超广角眼底图像）上几乎失效（AUROC 42.4%）。研究发现核心问题是结构不匹配：协变量偏移的输入在softmax空间中与分布内样本无法区分，但在VLM嵌入空间中占据不同区域。因此需要利用这一未开发的信号来改进OOD检测。

Method: 提出PROTON（原型基于的测试时在线OOD检测）方法：1）维护一个在线原型库，从高置信度的测试预测中提取；2）自适应融合原型距离与MCM评分，利用流级方差统计；3）无需模型修改、训练数据或提示工程。该方法通过比较输入在嵌入空间与原型的距离来检测分布外样本，特别适用于协变量偏移。

Result: 在眼科基准测试FLAIR + FIVES上，PROTON显著提升了MCM的OOD检测性能：协变量偏移上AUROC提升23.9分，语义偏移上提升8.8分，远分布偏移上提升8.1分。PROTON成为唯一能够在无需分层提示或标注数据的情况下，同时改进所有三种偏移类型的零样本OOD检测方法。

Conclusion: PROTON通过利用VLM嵌入空间中的原型距离信息，提供了一种轻量级、自适应的测试时OOD检测解决方案，显著提升了医疗VLMs在不同类型分布偏移上的检测能力，为实现临床部署中更可靠的异常检测迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot clinical image classification, yet reliably detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs at deployment remains an open problem. No static scoring method works across all shift types: Maximum Concept Matching (MCM) on FLAIR achieves 76.4% AUROC for far-OOD but only 42.4% for covariate shifts such as ultra-wide-field fundus images, effectively random. We trace this to a structural mismatch: covariate-shifted inputs are indistinguishable from in-distribution samples in softmax space, yet occupy distinct regions in the VLM embedding space. To exploit this untapped signal, we propose PROTON (PROtotype-based Test-time ONline OOD detection), a lightweight post-hoc module that maintains an online prototype bank from high-confidence test predictions and adaptively fuses prototype distance with MCM scoring via stream-level variance statistics, requiring no model modification, training data, or prompt engineering. On the ophthalmology benchmark FLAIR + FIVES, PROTON improves MCM by +23.9 AUROC on covariate shift, +8.8 on semantic shift, and +8.1 on far-OOD, making it the only zero-shot method to improve all three without hierarchical prompts or labeled data. Code is available at https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/PROTON, and the project page is available at https://genmi-lab.github.io/PROTON.

</details>


### [43] [GIM-ENDO: A Multimodal Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Gastric Intestinal Metaplasia Morphology and Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20919)
*Mojgan Forootan,Mahziar Setayeshfar,Ali Darvishi,Mohammad Tashakoripour,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GIM-ENDO是一个公开可用的胃黏膜肠化生(GIM)数据集，包含内镜图像、视频、组织病理学验证、幽门螺杆菌状态和详细的IEE内镜标注，旨在促进AI模型对GIM的实时检测和表征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃黏膜肠化生(GIM)是胃不典型增生和腺癌的前期病变，其早期检测对干预癌变级联反应至关重要。人工智能在GIM的实时内镜检测和表征方面具有巨大潜力，但可靠AI模型的开发受到缺乏公开可用的、经组织病理学验证的数据集的限制，这些数据集需要结合详细的内镜标注、组织学亚型(完全型和不完全型)、标准化分级系统和正常黏膜模式。

Method: GIM-ENDO数据集包含24名患者(22名GIM阳性，2名正常对照)的人口统计学数据、内镜发现、组织病理学结果和幽门螺杆菌状态。数据使用Olympus EVIS X1系统采集，包括白光内镜(WLE)和图像增强内镜(IEE)，如窄带成像(NBI)和放大NBI(M-NBI)。标注涵盖六个主要IEE内镜征象：浅蓝色嵴(LBC)、边缘混浊带(MTB)、白色不透明物质(WOS)、TV模式(融合)、萎缩和地图样红斑(MLE)，以及两个额外的内镜发现(AHP和GA)。所有GIM阳性病例都标注了GIM亚型(完全型和不完全型)，在完成组织学采样的病例中提供OLGA和OLGIM分期。

Result: 创建了一个全面、公开可用的GIM数据集(GIM-ENDO)，包含多模态数据：内镜图像、视频片段、组织病理学验证、详细的内镜征象标注、GIM亚型分类和标准化分期系统。数据集可通过https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20707267公开访问，相关工作已提交至MICCAI 2026 Open Data Track。

Conclusion: GIM-ENDO数据集填补了AI辅助GIM检测领域的数据空白，提供了一个全面、标准化、公开可用的资源，将促进可靠AI模型的开发，改进胃黏膜肠化生的实时内镜检测和表征，最终有助于早期胃癌预防。

Abstract: Gastric intestinal metaplasia (GIM) is a precursor lesion to gastric dysplasia and adenocarcinoma whose early detection is crucial for intervening in the carcinogenesis cascade. Artificial intelligence (AI) holds considerable promise for real-time endoscopic detection and characterization of GIM. However, development of reliable AI models has been constrained by the absence of publicly available, histopathologically validated datasets that combine detailed endoscopic annotations, histological subtype (complete and incomplete), standardized grading systems, and normal mucosal patterns. GIM-ENDO was designed to fill this gap. The dataset comprises demographic data, endoscopic findings, histopathological results, and H. pylori status acquired using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE) and image-enhanced endoscopy (IEE), including narrow-band imaging (NBI) and magnifying NBI (M-NBI), along with images and video clips from 24 patients (22 GIM-positive, 2 normal controls). Annotations cover six primary IEE endoscopic signs -- light blue crest (LBC), marginal turbid band (MTB), white opaque substance (WOS), TV pattern (Fusion), atrophy, and map-like erythema (MLE) -- plus two additional endoscopic findings (AHP and GA) recorded where present. GIM subtypes (complete and incomplete) are annotated for all GIM-positive cases; OLGA and OLGIM staging are provided where complete histological sampling was available. The dataset is publicly accessible at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20707267. For the latest updates and further information regarding this dataset, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: https://databiox.com
  A short version of this work has been submitted to MICCAI 2026 Open Data Track.

</details>


### [44] [ELDiff: When Evidential Learning Meets Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20924)
*Qingtao Pan,Kai Ye,Zhihao Dou,Bing Ji,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ELDiff是一个基于证据学习监督的文本到图像扩散模型，通过不确定性度量和冲突检测来增强多对象生成的语义一致性，无需额外推理时操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多对象文本到图像生成中，确保文本提示与生成视觉内容之间的语义一致性至关重要，但现有扩散模型在去噪过程中往往忽视这种一致性约束。虽然基于token监督的扩散模型可以通过学习图像内容与对象分割图之间的对象级一致性来缓解这一问题，但这类方法存在分割图偏置和语义重叠冲突的问题，特别是在涉及多个对象时。

Method: 提出了ELDiff模型，采用证据学习监督机制：1) 提出像素证据损失，通过证据正则化抑制对不可靠标签的过度自信；2) 设计token冲突损失，通过优化测得的冲突因子来弱化语义间的矛盾。该方法增强了对不可靠分割图的容错能力，并抑制语义冲突，从而强化对象级一致性学习。

Result: 在SD v1.4、SD v2.1、SDXL、SD v3.5和Qwen-Image等多个扩散模型上进行的广泛实验表明，ELDiff优于现有的基于训练和非训练方法的T2I扩散模型，且无需额外的推理时操作。

Conclusion: ELDiff能够无缝扩展到现有的T2I扩散模型训练流程中，通过证据学习和冲突检测机制有效解决了多对象生成中的语义一致性问题，为文本到图像生成提供了更加鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: In multi-object text-to-image (T2I) diffusion, ensuring semantic consistency between textual prompts and generated visual content is crucial for image synthesis. However, such consistency constraint is often underemphasized in the denoising process of diffusion models. Although token supervised diffusion models can mitigate this issue by learning object-wise consistency between the image content and object segmentation maps, it tends to suffer from the problems of segmentation map bias and semantic overlap conflict, especially when involving multiple objects. In this paper, we propose ELDiff, a new evidential learning-supervised T2I diffusion model, which leverages the advantages of uncertainty metric and conflict detection to enhance the fault tolerance of unreliable segmentation maps and suppress semantic conflicts, strengthening object-wise consistency learning. Specifically, a pixel evidence loss is proposed to restrain overconfidence in unreliable labels through evidential regularization, and a token conflict loss is designed to weaken the contradiction between semantics through optimizing a measured conflict factor. Extensive experiments show that our ELDiff outperforms existing training based and train-free based T2I diffusion models on SD v1.4, SD v2.1, SDXL, SD v3.5, and Qwen-Image, without requiring additional inference-time manipulations. Notably, ELDiff can be seamlessly extended to the existing training pipeline of T2I diffusion models. Code can be found at https://github.com/QingtaoPan/ELDiff.

</details>


### [45] [CogniRoute: Learning to Route Social Evidence in Omni-Modal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20970)
*Yifan Shen,Pei Tian,Xinzhuo Li,Bowen Fang,Shujun Xia,Bingxuan Li,Ana Jojic,Wenming Ye,Xu Cao,James Matthew Rehg,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogniRoute是一个基于认知模式指导的混合专家框架，用于社会全方位推理，通过在监督微调中使全局路由签名与认知结构对齐，并结合路由感知的强化学习，在OmniSocialBench上实现了59.38%的平均准确率，比最强基准有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全模态模型能够处理视频、音频和文本，但统一访问多模态并不能保证模型使用正确的证据。这一差距在社会视频问答中尤为明显，因为答案可能取决于手势、语音语调、时间线索或视听不匹配等因素。

Method: 提出了CogniRoute框架：1）使用仅训练时使用的认知模式，按跨模态关系、推理需求和时间范围分解每个示例；2）在监督微调中使全局路由签名与这种结构对齐；3）引入路由感知的强化学习，联合优化令牌生成和专家分配，使用答案正确性、模态一致推理和认知时间锚定的奖励信号。此外构建了OmniSocialBench数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: CogniRoute在OmniSocialBench上达到59.38%的平均准确率，比最强专有基线提高15.33个百分点，比最强开源全模态基线提高26.77个百分点，在需要视听协调、冲突解决和时间锚定的社会推理问题上改进最大。

Conclusion: CogniRoute通过认知模式指导的路由机制和强化学习优化，有效提升了全模态模型在社会视频问答中的推理能力，特别是在复杂的社会情境理解和多模态协调方面表现突出。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can ingest video, audio, and text, but unified access to multiple modalities does not guarantee that a model uses the right evidence. This gap is especially pronounced in social video question answering, where the answer may hinge on a gesture, vocal tone, temporal cue, or mismatch between what is said and what is visually expressed. We introduce CogniRoute, a schema-guided Mixture-of-Experts framework for social omni reasoning. CogniRoute uses a training-only cognitive schema that factorizes each example by cross-modal relation, reasoning demand, and temporal scope, and aligns global routing signatures with this structure during supervised fine-tuning. We further introduce route-aware reinforcement learning, which jointly optimizes token generation and expert allocation using rewards for answer correctness, modality-consistent reasoning, and cognitive temporal grounding. To support training and evaluation, we construct OmniSocialBench, a diagnostic social video QA resource with 118K structured training examples, grounded reasoning traces, schema labels, temporal evidence spans, and a manually verified evaluation split. CogniRoute achieves 59.38\% average accuracy on OmniSocialBench, improving over the strongest proprietary baseline by 15.33 percentage points and the strongest open-source omni baseline by 26.77 points, with the largest gains on questions requiring audio-visual coordination, conflict resolution, and temporally grounded social inference.

</details>


### [46] [UNITY: Attention Flow Networks for Adaptive Conditioning in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20971)
*Aryan Das,Koushik Biswas,Moloud Abdar,Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNITY是用于扩散图像生成的通用-专用适配器，通过两阶段训练实现多条件类型的共享语义学习，显著提高了内存效率和推理速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法为每种条件类型训练单独适配器效率低下，需要一种能够统一处理多种条件类型并能灵活适应单条件和复合条件设置的解决方案

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1）通用阶段使用一半训练步数学习跨条件模式的共享语义；2）专用化阶段使用剩余预算细化特定模式特征。核心组件包括可变形注意力流（MAF）网络和变形包装器模块，实现通道感知和空间自适应特征对齐

Result: 在多数据集上实验表明，UNITY在图像保真度方面达到最先进水平，同时显著降低推理延迟和内存消耗，支持单条件和复合条件下的灵活操作

Conclusion: UNITY提供了一种高效可扩展的复合条件适应方法，通过统一的训练框架和创新的架构组件，在保持性能的同时大幅提升了效率，为扩散模型的实用化部署提供了有力工具

Abstract: We introduce UNITY, a Universal-to-Specialized adapter for efficient and scalable composite conditioning in diffusion based image generation. Unlike prior methods that train separate adapters for each conditioning modality, UNITY jointly learns shared semantics across multiple conditioning types and subsequently specializes without modifying the underlying architecture. The proposed two stage training paradigm consists of a Universal Stage that captures cross modal representations across all conditioning modalities using half of the total training steps, followed by a Specialization Stage that refines modality specific features using the remaining training budget. At the core of UNITY are the Morphable Attention Flow (MAF) Network and Morph Wrapper modules, which enable channel aware and spatially adaptive feature alignment through learnable flow fields and attention based fusion. This constant complexity formulation supports flexible operation under both single and composite conditioning settings while significantly reducing inference latency and memory consumption. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that UNITY achieves state of the art image fidelity while maintaining superior memory efficiency. Code: https://github.com/arya-domain/UNITY

</details>


### [47] [Robusto-2: Benchmarking Humans & VLMs for Autonomous Driving in Lima & New York City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20980)
*Adrian Cespedes,Marcelo Chincha,Dunant Cusipuma,Victor Flores-Benites,David Ortega,Arturo Deza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究自动驾驶汽车中视觉语言模型（VLMs）在新地理环境中的泛化能力，通过对比利马和纽约市的人类驾驶员与VLMs对两地行车记录仪视频的反应差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶汽车国际化扩展并使用多模态系统（如VLMs）作为其行为模型的认知骨干，需要评估这些系统在新环境中的泛化能力，特别是在新地理区域中的分布外（OOD）边缘案例场景

Method: 采用完全析因分析设计，对比利马人类驾驶员、纽约市人类驾驶员和VLMs，向他们展示来自利马和纽约市的行车记录仪视频，通过视觉问答范式提出四类问题：事实性、评级、反事实和推理问题

Result: 人类和VLMs的回答存在差异，但这种差异受问题类型调节；不同地区的人类驾驶员回答相似；出乎意料的是，未发现地理因素对人类或VLMs回答有显著调节作用，可能是由于场景的分布外性质

Conclusion: 地理因素对人类和VLMs的交通场景理解影响有限，可能因为测试场景具有高度分布外特性；人类跨地域反应一致，而VLMs与人类存在系统性差异，这种差异随问题类型变化

Abstract: As Self-Driving Cars continue to expand internationally and use multi-modal systems such as VLMs as a cognitive backbone for their Action models; how well will these systems generalize in new settings, in particular out-of-distribution (OOD) edge-case scenarios in new geographies? In this paper, we study this open question by providing a full factorial analysis with human drivers of Lima, human drivers from New York City, and VLMs and showing them dashcam footage collected from Lima and New York City -- prompting them with a variety of questions under a Visual Question Answering (VQA) paradigm. In particular, we pick these two cities as they are highly challenging driving locations where no Self-Driving Car company currently operates in, and ask questions that span 4 categories: Factual, Ratings, Counterfactual and Reasoning. We find that Humans and VLMs diverge in their responses -- though this is modulated by the type of questions asked, and that Humans answer similarly independent of where they are from (Lima/NYC). To our surprise, we did not find a strong difference in terms of answers (Humans or VLMs) that was modulated by geography, likely due to their high out-of-distribution nature. Our dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Artificio/robusto-2

</details>


### [48] [CheXpercept: A Benchmark for Evaluating Expert-Level Lesion Perception in Chest X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21020)
*Geon Choi,Hangyul Yoon,Nalee Kim,Jeong Yun Jang,Hyunju Shin,Hyunki Park,Sang Hoon Seo,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CheXpercept是一个用于评估视觉-语言模型在胸部X光分析中的多级感知能力的基准，模拟放射科医生的认知工作流程，包含粗粒度检测、细粒度轮廓评估与修订、语义级属性提取三个层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型在胸部X光分析中的评估大多局限于疾病存在性分类，缺乏视觉定位验证，无法确保模型具备专家级的病灶感知能力，从而影响了临床可靠性。

Method: 开发了CheXpercept基准，包含三个顺序感知层级：1）粗粒度检测（病灶存在与否），2）细粒度轮廓评估与修订（边界框和分割掩码），3）语义级属性提取（形态、位置等特征）。数据集通过半自动化生成流程构建，并由6名医学专家审核，包含从2,100张胸部X光片中提取的10,400个问答项，涵盖7种临床关键肺部和心脏病灶。

Result: 对14个通用和医学专用视觉-语言模型的基准测试显示：模型仅在粗粒度检测层面表现尚可，而在更深层视觉任务上性能急剧下降。值得注意的是，医学专用模型几乎没有表现出相对于通用模型的感知优势，揭示了当前领域适应方法的系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: CheXpercept基准暴露了当前视觉-语言模型在胸部X光分析中的严重感知缺陷，尤其是在细粒度和语义级任务上。医学专用模型的优势不明显，表明需要开发更有效的领域适应方法来提升临床感知能力。该基准和数据集将公开可用以推动相关研究。

Abstract: The evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) for chest X-ray (CXR) analysis has largely been limited to disease-presence classification without visual grounding. Such evaluations fail to verify the expert-level lesion perception necessary to ensure the clinical reliability of VLMs. To address these limitations, we introduce CheXpercept, a sequential, multi-level perception benchmark that mirrors a radiologist's cognitive workflow across coarse-level detection, fine-level contour evaluation and revision, and semantic-level attribute extraction. To ensure high clinical fidelity at scale, we construct the dataset using a semi-automated generation pipeline paired with a review by six medical experts. CheXpercept contains 10,400 QA items derived from 2,100 CXRs, covering seven clinically critical pulmonary and cardiac lesions. To demonstrate the current landscape of VLM perception, we benchmark 14 general and medical VLMs on CheXpercept. The models achieve adequate performance only at the coarse level, with accuracy degrading precipitously on deeper visual tasks. Notably, medical VLMs show almost no perceptual advantage over their general-domain counterparts, highlighting a systemic flaw in current domain adaptation. The code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [49] [Sparse Point-Guided Fusion of Supervised and Self-Supervised Learning Model for Seaweed Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21026)
*Tatsuya Suzuki,Kazuya Ijuin,Hideki Tomimori,Megumi Chikano,Katsushi Sakai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于蓝碳量化的两阶段海藻分割方法，结合监督学习和自监督学习，解决水下图像复杂性和数据稀缺问题，在海藻分类精度上有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋在可持续发展中起关键作用，蓝碳生态系统是重要的天然碳汇。准确的海藻分类对蓝碳量化至关重要，但传统监督学习方法受数据稀缺和领域差距限制，自监督学习无法分配类别标签，难以处理水下复杂环境和多样海藻物种。

Method: 提出新颖的两阶段海藻分割技术：1) SSL.Prop.：利用监督学习获取初始类别信息和大致位置，指导自监督学习进行精确详细分割；2) MaskFusion (MF)：通过合并实例级掩模进一步细化结果。该方法实现自动类别标签分配并减轻领域差距影响，具体流程是实例分割估计稀疏点位置，然后指导自监督学习进行详细区域分割。

Result: 使用山口县水下图像进行评估，完整方法(SSL.Prop.+MF)相比USIS-SAM实现了0.068 mIoU的提升，表现出显著精度增益，特别在小海藻分类上效果明显。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了改善蓝碳量化和海洋生态系统监测的强大潜力，为解决水下图像分析中的挑战提供了有效方案，有助于推进海洋数字孪生计划中的精确生态系统监测。

Abstract: The ocean plays a critical role in sustainable development, particularly in climate change mitigation. Among marine ecosystems, blue carbon ecosystems are recognized as important natural carbon sinks. In this context, this paper addresses precise seaweed classification for blue carbon quantification in Ocean Digital Twin initiatives. Conventional methods, including supervised learning (limited by data scarcity and domain gaps) and self-supervised learning (unable to assign class labels), struggle with underwater complexities and diverse seaweed species. To overcome this, we propose a novel two-stage seaweed segmentation technique. This technique first utilizes Supervised and Self-supervised Learning Model Propagation (SSL.Prop.), which leverages supervised learning for initial class information and approximate locations, guiding self-supervised learning for detailed, accurate segmentation. Subsequently, MaskFusion (MF) refines these results by merging instance-level masks for highly accurate segmentation. This integrated approach allows automatic class label assignment and mitigates domain gap effects. Specifically, instance segmentation estimates sparse point locations which then guide self-supervised learning for detailed region segmentation. Evaluated with underwater images from Yamaguchi Prefecture, our full proposed method (SSL.Prop.+MF) achieved a 0.068 mIoU improvement over USIS-SAM, demonstrating significant accuracy gains, particularly for small seaweed. This approach demonstrates strong potential for improving blue carbon quantification and marine ecosystem monitoring.

</details>


### [50] [Self-Supervised Dual-Frequency Phase Decomposition for Single-Shot Composite Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21027)
*Jin-Hyuk Seok,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于单次复合条纹图案的自监督相位细化框架，无需相位或深度标签，通过利用高低频相位梯度关系改进相位分离可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）在实时测量、动态物体重建和运动敏感环境中具有重要应用价值。现有方法主要依赖傅里叶变换或监督深度学习，但傅里叶变换方法精度有限且在复杂区域性能下降，监督方法需要昂贵的相位或深度标签

Method: 提出自监督相位细化框架：1）利用低频和高频相位梯度之间的尺度和方向关系，提高相位分离可靠性；2）引入软边缘一致性损失函数，保留物体边界和精细几何结构；3）无需相位或深度地面真实标签

Result: 实验结果显示：MAE_z=0.367 mm，RMSE_z=1.804 mm，优于最佳变换基准方法（0.402 mm和2.785 mm）；有效像素比从84.75%提高到95.07%

Conclusion: 该方法证明了自监督双频相位细化在不依赖地面真实标签监督的情况下实现可靠单次3D重建的有效性，为解决单次FPP中的相位模糊问题提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been actively studied for real-time measurement, dynamic object reconstruction, and motion-sensitive environments. Composite fringe patterns are advantageous in single-shot FPP because multiple frequency components can be encoded in a single pattern, enabling phase ambiguity resolution. Existing approaches mainly rely on Fourier transform-based methods or supervised deep learning methods. However, Fourier transform-based methods often suffer from limited accuracy and degraded performance in complex regions, while supervised methods require dense phase or depth labels, which are costly to obtain. In this work, we propose a self-supervised phase refinement framework for single-shot composite fringe patterns without requiring phase or depth labels. The proposed method exploits the scale and direction relationships between low- and high-frequency phase gradients, improving the reliability of phase separation. We also introduce a soft edge consistency loss to preserve object boundaries and fine geometric structures. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves MAE_z and RMSE_z of 0.367 mm and 1.804 mm, respectively, outperforming the best-performing transform-based baseline, which obtains 0.402 mm and 2.785 mm. The proposed method also improves the valid-pixel ratio from 84.75 % to 95.07 %. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of self-supervised dual-frequency phase refinement for reliable single-shot 3D reconstruction without ground-truth label supervision.

</details>


### [51] [Neural Architecture Distributions: A New Paradigm for Stochastic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21061)
*Conghui Li,Junhao Huang,Chern Hong Lim,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过架构分布实现随机分割的新方法：不是采样潜变量或噪声，而是从分割主干中可搜索位置的算子选择分布中采样离散架构，每个采样架构通过选择的活跃路径生成一个掩码，实现输出多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机分割需要在单张图像中生成多个合理的掩码，这在医疗成像或建筑缺陷检测等安全关键应用中至关重要。现有方法通常通过注入连续潜变量或迭代去噪轨迹引入随机性，但这些随机源难以直接搜索或审计。

Method: 1. 提出架构分布作为分割的新随机源：采样离散架构而非潜变量或噪声；2. 训练使用集合级监督，通过基于IoU的能量距离代理将架构采样预测集与标注集匹配；3. 通过进化搜索构建候选库，在分布学习前优化随机源的支撑集。

Result: 在LIDC-IDRI数据集上实现了最先进的分布匹配和假设覆盖，并在两个扩展任务上保持有效。这是首次将随机分割形式化为学习架构分布并通过架构采样实现输出多样性的工作。

Conclusion: 通过架构分布实现随机分割提供了一种可审计的随机源，支持架构溯源，每个输出对应特定的架构配置，避免了传统方法中随机源难以搜索和审计的问题，为安全关键应用提供了更可靠和可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Stochastic segmentation seeks to represent multiple plausible masks for a single image, which is essential in safety- and quality-critical applications such as medical imaging or building defect inspection. Most existing methods introduce stochasticity by injecting continuous latent variables or by iterative denoising trajectories, whose stochastic sources are difficult to search or audit directly. We propose architecture distributions as a new stochastic source for segmentation: instead of sampling a latent variable or noise, we sample a discrete architecture from a learned distribution over operator choices at multiple searchable positions in a segmentation backbone. Each sampled architecture yields one mask through the selected active path, so inference depends on the executed subnet rather than the complete candidate bank. This approach also supports architectural provenance, since each output corresponds to a specific architecture configuration. To reduce collapse toward averaged masks, we train with set-level supervision by matching a set of architecture-sampled predictions to the annotation set using an IoU-based energy-distance surrogate. We further construct the candidate bank with evolutionary search, making the support of the stochastic source optimizable before distribution learning. The proposed method achieves state-of-the-art distribution matching and hypothesis coverage on LIDC-IDRI, and remains effective on two extension tasks. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to formulate stochastic segmentation as learning an architecture distribution and realizing output diversity through architecture sampling.

</details>


### [52] [ShuffleFlow: Scalable Posterior Inference for Bayesian Inverse Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21099)
*Tianao Li,Tjitske Starkenburg,Yu Sun,Emma Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShuffleFlow：一种用于大规模科学成像反问题的可扩展变分推理框架，通过像素重排、神经场和条件归一化流来高效建模后验分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分推理在科学成像反问题中很有效，但基于流的网络可扩展性差，限制了其在大规模图像重建中的应用

Method: 提出ShuffleFlow框架，包含三个组件：1)基于像素重排的图像坐标采样器；2)作为特征编码器的神经场；3)作为后验估计器的条件归一化流。通过像素重排将图像分割为子图像栈，使用共享的条件归一化流建模子图像栈的联合分布

Result: 在线性和非线性成像反问题上展示了方法的有效性和效率，能够比扩散采样器更快地生成高样本计数的后验分布

Conclusion: ShuffleFlow解决了传统变分推理的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模成像反问题的后验推理提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Variational inference (VI) is a powerful method for principled posterior inference for scientific inverse imaging. VI learns the posterior distribution, often with a flow-based network, which can cheaply generate posterior samples upon optimization, and can flexibly incorporate score-based or classic priors. However, its application to large-scale image reconstruction is severely hindered by the poor scalability of the flow-based networks. In this work, we introduce ShuffleFlow, a scalable VI framework to address this challenge. Our method breaks down the problem into three parts: a pixel-unshuffling-based image coordinate sampler, a neural field as feature encoder, and a conditional normalizing flow (CNF) as posterior estimator. Specifically, our framework partitions an image into a stack of sub-images with pixel-unshuffling and uses a shared CNF to model the joint distribution of the sub-image stack. We condition the CNF on the output of a neural field, which embeds feature vectors corresponding to pixel-unshuffling sample locations to capture spatial structures, and share the flow's latent variable across the channels to model their correlations. We demonstrate our method's effectiveness and efficiency on both linear and nonlinear imaging inverse problems, and show its ability to more rapidly generate a high-sample-count posterior than diffusion samplers.

</details>


### [53] [SARIF: Segment Anything for Robust Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21108)
*Dong-Hyun Moon,Ju-Hyeon Nam,Sang-Chul Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SARIF框架，利用Segment Anything Model的强泛化能力，通过反馈引导的掩码解码器和双编码器设计，实现无需人工输入的图像伪造区域自动定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像伪造定位面临多样化篡改技术和分布偏移的挑战。现有模型在基准测试上表现良好，但在跨域泛化和鲁棒性方面存在不足，需要更稳健的解决方案

Method: 1. 基于Segment Anything Model的SARIF框架，利用其可提示架构和强泛化能力
2. 引入反馈引导的掩码解码器和双编码器设计提取伪造特定信息
3. 设计块级提示机制，从自适应编码器与其冻结对应部分的残差特征中获取伪造线索
4. 特征与前一掩码提示融合，驱动基于反馈的掩码细化过程

Result: 在标准伪造定位基准测试上，SARIF实现了强大的平均跨数据集性能，并对常见图像损坏具有鲁棒性

Conclusion: SARIF框架通过结合SAM的强大泛化能力和伪造特定的特征提取机制，有效解决了图像伪造定位中的跨域泛化和鲁棒性问题，实现了无需人工干预的自动伪造区域分割

Abstract: Image forgery localization remains challenging due to diverse manipulation techniques and distribution shifts. Existing forgery localization models achieve high accuracy on benchmarks but often struggle with cross-domain generalization and robustness. In this paper, we propose SARIF (Segment Anything for Robust Image Forensics), a framework that leverages the Segment Anything Model (SAM), which has a promptable architecture and strong generalization ability. SARIF introduces a feedback-guided mask decoder and a dual-encoder design that extracts forgery-specific information to capture forensic traces while exploiting the SAM architecture. To localize manipulated regions, we design a block-wise prompting mechanism that derives forgery-specific cues from residual features between an adapted encoder and its frozen counterpart. These features are fused with the previous mask prompt to drive a feedback-based mask refinement process, enabling automatic forgery segmentation without manual input. Extensive experiments on standard forgery-localization benchmarks show that SARIF achieves strong average cross-dataset performance and robustness to common image corruptions.

</details>


### [54] [Object-Centric Dataset Resources for Constrained-Data Image Generation and Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21113)
*Vasile Marian,Yong-Bin Kang,Alexander Buddery*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发布了一个面向对象中心图像生成的可共享数据集集合，包含Cityscapes-Pedestrian、TrafficSigns和COCO PottedPlant三个资源，标准化了256×256的对象中心裁剪和边界框标注，支持低数据条件下的对象中心图像生成和数据增强方法评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能城市行人分析、交通标志检查、领域特定目标检测等少标签样本场景中，对象中心图像生成很重要。合成图像在训练和评估时最有用的数据集应当保留对象结构、边界框、视觉多样性和真实上下文。现有图像数据集通常面向分类、检测或场景理解，而不是针对有限类特定数据的可控对象中心生成和增强。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个可共享的对象中心数据集集合，包含三个资源：Cityscapes-Pedestrian（密集行人场景）、TrafficSigns（高对比度交通标志）、COCO PottedPlant（多样化盆栽植物场景）。这些数据集标准化了256×256的对象中心裁剪和边界框标注，提供了直接数据、脚本、清单、边界框级标注表、校验和以及重建文档。通过GitHub和Zenodo平台公开发布。

Result: 数据集集合包含3,009个TrafficSigns样本，2,156个Cityscapes-Pedestrian清单记录，以及7,679个COCO PottedPlant清单记录。COCO衍生的清单保留了上下文和多实例多样性，同时可以通过固定随机种子抽取等大小子集进行受控比较。提供了完整的重建文档和支持材料。

Conclusion: 该数据集集合支持标签和分割检查、子集创建、上游数据重建，并在共享记录上评估对象中心图像生成或合成数据增强方法，为解决少标签场景下的对象中心图像生成问题提供了标准化的基准资源。

Abstract: Object-centric image generation is important in settings with few labeled examples, including pedestrian analysis in smart-city scenes, traffic-sign inspection, and domain-specific object detection. Synthetic images are most useful for training and evaluation when datasets preserve object structure, bounding boxes, visual diversity, and realistic context. Existing image datasets usually target classification, detection, or scene understanding rather than controlled object-centric generation and augmentation with limited class-specific data. We present a shareable collection of three object-centric dataset resources: Cityscapes-Pedestrian, TrafficSigns, and COCO PottedPlant. The collection standardizes 256-by-256 object-centric crops and bounding-box annotations across three regimes: dense pedestrian scenes with privacy blur and occlusion, cleaner high-contrast traffic signs, and context-diverse potted-plant scenes. The release contains 3,009 TrafficSigns samples, 2,156 Cityscapes-Pedestrian manifest records, and 7,679 COCO PottedPlant manifest records. The larger COCO-derived manifest preserves contextual and multi-instance diversity, while equal-size subsets can be drawn with a fixed random seed for controlled comparisons. The release provides direct TrafficSigns data where redistribution is permitted, together with scripts, manifests, box-level annotation tables, checksums, and reconstruction documentation for the Cityscapes- and COCO-derived subsets. It is available through the Latzi/object-centric-low-data-datasets GitHub repository and Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20573001. The collection supports label and split inspection, subset creation, reconstruction from upstream data, and evaluation of object-centric image generation or synthetic-data augmentation methods on shared records.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [Less is More: Lightweight Prompt Compression for Question Answering Applications on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20571)
*Zihuai Xu,Ruofei Hou,Yang Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Ying Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORE是一种两阶段句子级提示压缩方法，用于消除基于SLM的方法在边缘设备上部署时的内存和计算开销，通过NER和语义匹配构建答案集和线索集，显著提升效率并减少能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理驱动的QA应用中，RAG通过提供额外上下文增强LLM响应准确性。然而，检索结果的固有噪声和文档级检索的粗粒度导致检索到的上下文包含大量冗余信息，造成LLM推理时不必要的计算开销。现有的提示压缩方法依赖辅助SLM估计上下文重要性，但引入了显著的内存和计算开销，限制了其在资源受限边缘设备上的部署。

Method: 提出CORE两阶段句子级提示压缩方法：第一阶段通过命名实体识别（NER）构建答案集，通过语义匹配构建线索集；第二阶段使用正交残差检索策略精炼线索集，设计基于空间邻近度的指标过滤答案集。然后将两个集合结合形成最终压缩上下文。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin边缘设备和华为Nova智能手机上实现。在2000-token预算内，CORE相比最先进基线至少提升30.19%准确率，同时减少至少50.47%内存使用，在边缘设备上实现至少1.94倍加速。相比最先进的LLMLingua2方法，在智能手机上实现95.74%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: CORE通过消除对辅助SLM的依赖，显著降低了提示压缩的内存和计算开销，提高了边缘设备上的部署可行性，同时保持甚至提升了问答准确性，具有实际应用价值和广泛的移动部署通用性。

Abstract: In agent-driven question answering (QA) applications, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly introduced to enhance the response accuracy of large language models (LLMs) by providing additional context. Due to the inherent noise in retrieval results and the coarse granularity of document-level retrieval, the retrieved context often contains substantial redundant information. In this setting, the agent prompt, consisting of the user query and the associated retrieved context, leads to unnecessary computational overhead during LLM inference. Existing prompt compression methods typically rely on auxiliary small language models (SLMs) to estimate context importance. However, such approaches introduce significant memory and computational overhead, which limits their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. In this paper, we propose CORE, a two-stage sentence-level prompt compression method that eliminates the need for SLMs. In the first stage, CORE constructs an answer set via named entity recognition (NER) and a clue set via semantic matching. In the second stage, CORE refines the clue set using an orthogonal residual retrieval strategy and designs a spatial proximity-based metric to filter the answer set. The two sets are then combined to form the final compressed context. We implement CORE on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin edge device and a Huawei Nova smartphone. Experimental results demonstrate that within a 2000-token budget, CORE improves accuracy by at least 30.19% compared to state-of-the-art baselines, while reducing memory usage by at least 50.47% and achieving at least 1.94 times speedup on the edge device. Moreover, compared to the state-of-the-art LLMLingua2 method, CORE achieves a substantial energy reduction of 95.74% on the smartphone, highlighting its practicality and generalizability for mobile deployments.

</details>


### [56] [Investigating Linguistic Steering: An Analysis of Adjectival Effects Across Large Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20572)
*Lars Malmqvist*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用Shapley值量化形容词对LLM表现的引导效应，发现：1)少数形容词有不成比例的强大影响，但效果非普适；2)模型家族效应显著；3)形容词效果高度依赖句法位置；4)大模型存在强烈的非加性交互效应，小模型响应更字面化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对LLM语言提示控制缺乏精确、可扩展的理解，主要依赖经验性启发式方法。需要建立原则性归因框架来量化语言线索对模型性能的引导效果，以支持更可靠的AI对齐。

Method: 引入基于Shapley值的严谨框架，量化单个形容词对模型表现的引导效应。该方法应用于MMLU基准测试上的100个形容词，覆盖多样化模型套件(o3, gpt-4o-mini, phi-3, llama-3-70b, deepseek-r1)，并进行跨模型分析和详细后续研究。

Result: 发现少数形容词作为"杠杆"具有不成比例的强大引导作用，但效果非普适。模型家族效应明显：相同谱系模型呈现相关敏感性模式，而架构不同的模型反应基本不相关。形容词的引导方向高度依赖其在提示中的句法角色和位置。大模型(gpt-4o-mini)表现出强烈的非加性交互效应(协同放大、对抗抑制甚至反转效应)，而小模型(phi-3)响应更字面化且缺乏组合性。

Conclusion: 模型规模增大时，对提示的理解更复杂但也更不可预测，这对鲁棒引导模型行为构成显著挑战。研究强调了需要开发组合性和模型特定的对齐技术，挑战了"一刀切"提示策略的假设。

Abstract: Achieving reliable control of Large Language Models (LLMs) requires a precise, scalable understanding of how they interpret linguistic cues. We introduce a rigorous framework using Shapley values to quantify the steering effect of individual adjectives on model performance, moving beyond anecdotal heuristics to principled attribution. Applying this method to 100 adjectives across a diverse suite of models (including o3, gpt-4o-mini, phi-3, llama-3-70b, and deepseek-r1) on the MMLU benchmark, we uncover several critical findings for AI alignment. First, we find that a small subset of adjectives act as disproportionately powerful "levers," yet their effects are not universal. Cross-model analysis reveals a "family effect": models of a shared lineage exhibit correlated sensitivity profiles, while architecturally distinct models react in a largely uncorrelated manner, challenging the notion of a one-size-fits-all prompting strategy. Second, focused follow-up studies demonstrate that the steering direction of these powerful adjectives is not intrinsic but is highly contingent on their syntactic role and position within the prompt. For larger models like gpt-4o-mini, we provide the first quantitative evidence of strong, non-additive interaction effects where adjectives can synergistically amplify, antagonistically dampen, or even reverse each other's impact. In contrast, smaller models like phi-3 exhibit a more literal and less compositional response. These results suggest that as models scale, their interpretation of prompts becomes more sophisticated but also less predictable, posing a significant challenge for robustly steering model behavior and highlighting the need for compositional and model-specific alignment techniques.

</details>


### [57] [Post-Training Recipe, More Than Model Family, Shapes Multi-Agent LLM Conversational Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20632)
*Luyang Zhang,Jialu Wang,Fei Xue,Yi-Yun Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在多LLM系统中，后训练配方比模型家族标签更能预测对话行为的多样性，仅依赖家族标签会导致对行为相似性的错误判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在多LLM交互系统中（这是实际部署系统的真实场景），模型家族标签是否真能预测对话行为多样性，因为离线研究表明LLM在单独评估时偏好同家族输出，但这对交互系统的适用性尚未测试。

Method: 使用940,000条链11个检查点的语料库和160万条链的同基础Llama因子实验，通过已验证的头部指标（hedging）分析行为差异，并进行跨家族对比和控制子集分析。

Result: 推理蒸馏的Llama检查点根据合作伙伴的不同会产生18%的hedging变化，超过控制子集中的任何跨家族hedging差距；Qwen、闭源API和运行时检查表明这一模式并非孤立现象。

Conclusion: 后训练配方是多LLM组合配置的首要维度，而仅依赖模型家族标签是不完整的对话多样性代理指标。

Abstract: Multi-LLM systems use multiple language models to deliberate, judge each other's outputs, or coordinate as agents. Their value depends on the models producing measurably different conversational behaviors when given the same input. Prior offline studies recommend drawing one model per family for behavioral diversity, because LLMs prefer outputs from their own family when rating one another in isolation. Whether the same family label predicts behavior in interactive multi-LLM systems, the setting that real deployed systems use, has not been tested. We study this with a 940,000-chain 11-checkpoint corpus and a 1.6M-chain same-base Llama factorial. On our validated headline metric, hedging, a reasoning-distilled Llama checkpoint shifts by 18% depending on which same-base partner it replies to, more than any cross-family hedging gap in the controlled subset. Qwen, closed-API, and runtime checks suggest the pattern is not isolated, while repair and challenge analyses remain exploratory because their surface-cue detectors are weaker. Overall, the results identify post-training recipe as a first-class axis for multi-LLM panel composition and show that model family alone is an incomplete proxy for conversational diversity.

</details>


### [58] [EmoInstruct-TTS: Dual-Path Instruction-Guided Emotional Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20650)
*Minghui Wu,Ganjun Liu,Zikun Fang,Ting Meng,Hongchuan Wu,Bingao Xu,Yonglong Cai,Jiasheng Chen,Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmoInstruct-TTS: 双路径指令引导的情感语音合成框架，通过Emotion2embed细粒度情感嵌入和ICE-Flow模型实现自由指令驱动的强度可控情感语音合成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令可控语音合成方法通常依赖粗糙的情感标签，缺乏对细粒度情感强度的显式建模，无法满足用户通过自然语言精确指定情感强度和细微差别的需求。

Method: 提出EmoInstruct-TTS双路径框架，包含：1) Emotion2embed：监督式语义-声学情感嵌入，涵盖48种情感状态（含细粒度类别和强度等级）；2) ICE-Flow：指令条件情感流模型，从自由形式指令推断声学基础的情感表示；3) LLM基合成管道，集成推断的嵌入以实现显式情感控制同时保持语义规划。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比强基线方法，EmoInstruct-TTS在情感可控性和语音自然度方面均有显著改进。

Conclusion: EmoInstruct-TTS通过细粒度情感嵌入和指令条件推断的集成，为指令驱动的情感语音合成提供了更精确的控制能力，在保持语义规划的同时提升了情感表达的自然性和可控性。

Abstract: Instruction-based controllable speech synthesis enables users to specify emotions through natural language. However, existing approaches often rely on coarse emotion labels and lack explicit modeling of fine-grained intensity. We propose EmoInstruct-TTS, a dual-path instruction-guided framework for emotional speech synthesis. We introduce Emotion2embed, a supervised semantic-acoustic emotion embedding covering 48 emotional states, including fine-grained categories and intensity levels. To infer embeddings from free-form instructions, we design an Instruction-Conditioned Emotion Flow Model (ICE-Flow) that generates acoustically grounded emotion representations. The inferred embeddings are integrated into an LLM-based synthesis pipeline to provide explicit emotional control while preserving semantic planning. Experiments show improved emotional controllability and speech naturalness over strong baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Specific Domain Ontology Construction Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20691)
*Vivian Magri Alcaldi Soares,Renata Wassermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种利用大语言模型作为领域专家自动构建概念层次结构的方法，并在巴西海上领土（蓝亚马逊）领域进行了实验验证，比较了GPT-3.5和GPT-4的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体是组织和管理信息的重要结构，但手动构建本体耗时耗力，导致许多特定领域缺乏参考本体。大语言模型在自然语言理解方面的卓越能力为辅助本体开发提供了新机遇。

Method: 采用一种技术使用LLMs作为领域专家角色，为给定初始概念构建概念层次结构。在巴西海上领土（蓝亚马逊）领域自动构建了20个本体，分别使用GPT-3.5和GPT-4模型，然后由人类专家进行评估。

Result: 模型能够构建总体上连贯的领域概念化，但没有任何输出结果在未经精炼的情况下完全满意地表示上下文。所有自动生成的本体都需要进一步改进才能作为领域参考本体。

Conclusion: LLMs在自动本体构建方面展示了潜力，能够生成基本连贯的概念层次结构，但仍需要人类专家的监督和精炼才能获得高质量的本体，不能完全替代领域专家。

Abstract: Ontologies are useful structures to organize and maintain information that can be understood both by humans and systems. However, since their manual crafting is a laborious task, many specific domains lack reference ontologies. The outstanding ability for understanding natural language demonstrated by the Large Language Models (LLMs) has motivated their application to aid on a variety of fields, including on ontology development. This work presents the experimentation with a technique that uses LLMs in the role of domain experts to build conceptual hierarchies for a given initial concept. Twenty ontologies automatically constructed for the domain of the Brazilian maritime territory (a.k.a the Blue Amazon) using GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 were then evaluated by human experts. The models were able to construct overall coherent conceptualizations of the domain, but none of the outputs was completely satisfactory as a representation of the context without refinement.

</details>


### [60] [MindAlign: Decoding Inner Speech from fMRI Signals via Multimodal Embedding Alignment under Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20696)
*Muxuan Liu,Ichiro Kobayashi,Satoshi Nishida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MindAlign是一个两阶段大脑到语言框架，通过将fMRI信号映射到共享多模态语义空间，然后使用冻结的多模态语言模型进行开放文本生成，实现了无需修改基础语言模型的脑信号解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从非侵入性脑信号解码内部言语（inner speech）的基本挑战，包括缺乏明确语言输出、训练数据有限以及个体间差异大。现有方法通常依赖于任务特定的解码器微调，限制了可扩展性并使得适应新参与者变得复杂。

Method: 提出MindAlign，一个解耦的两阶段框架：第一阶段学习特定主体的神经-语义对齐，将fMRI活动映射到共享多模态语义空间，提取内部生成句子的潜在语义草图；第二阶段将该草图与视觉上下文整合，提示冻结的多模态语言模型进行自由生成。

Result: 在静默图像描述的fMRI数据实验中，该方法在一致性和性能上优于仅使用fMRI和随机基线。学习到的语义到语言投影能够跨主体泛化，当与特定主体神经对齐配对时能够实现有效解码。

Conclusion: 神经信号调节超越图像驱动的先验的语义内容，支持了大脑到文本解码的可扩展和模块化方向，为内部言语解码提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Decoding inner speech from non-invasive brain signals remains a fundamental challenge due to the absence of overt linguistic output, limited training data, and large inter-subject variability. Existing brain-to-text approaches often rely on task-specific decoder fine-tuning, which restricts scalability and complicates adaptation to new participants. We propose MindAlign, a decoupled two-stage brain-to-language framework that enables open-ended text generation from fMRI signals without modifying the underlying language model. The first stage learns a subject-specific neural-semantic alignment that maps fMRI activity into a shared multimodal semantic space, extracting a latent semantic sketch of the internally generated sentence. The second stage integrates this sketch with visual context to prompt a frozen multimodal language model for free-form generation. Experiments on fMRI data collected during silent image description demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms fMRI-only and random baselines. We further show that the learned semantic-to-language projection can generalize across subjects, enabling effective decoding when paired with subject-specific neural alignment. These results indicate that neural signals modulate semantic content beyond image-driven priors, supporting a scalable and modular direction for brain-to-text decoding.

</details>


### [61] [VeriBound: PAC-Bayesian Generalization Bounds for Process Reward Models Trained with Formal Verification Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20740)
*Amirul Rahman,Mohammed Sabih Alsharari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VeriBound：一个为使用形式验证工具训练的PRM提供PAC-Bayesian泛化界限的理论框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过程奖励模型（PRMs）训练数据获取存在瓶颈：人工标注成本高，蒙特卡洛方法噪声大。虽然FOVER方法使用形式验证工具自动标注步骤级错误标签并观察到跨任务泛化现象，但缺乏理论解释，没有正式的泛化误差、样本复杂度、收敛率或下游Best-of-K性能界限。

Method: 提出VeriBound理论框架，基于PAC-Bayesian理论为使用形式验证工具训练的PRMs提供泛化界限。具体包括：(1)PAC-Bayesian泛化界限，将形式验证标注训练数据上的经验验证误差与未见推理任务上的期望误差联系起来；(2)样本复杂度分析；(3)收敛性分析；(4)误差传播界限。

Result: 建立了四个主要理论结果：(i)PAC-Bayesian泛化界限，依赖于形式验证准确率和训练-测试任务分布差异；(ii)样本复杂度为O(d log(d/δ)/ε²)；(iii)在L-光滑性和有界方差条件下，PRM训练以线性速率收敛；(iv)步骤级验证误差与Best-of-K性能退化关系的误差传播界限。

Conclusion: VeriBound为使用形式验证工具训练的PRMs提供了首个理论保障框架，解决了现有方法缺乏理论支撑的问题，为跨任务泛化现象提供了数学解释，并为实际应用提供了可计算的界限。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) provide step-level verification for Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, yet their training data acquisition remains a bottleneck: human annotation is costly and Monte Carlo roll-out estimates are noisy. A recent approach, FOVER, trains PRMs on step-level error labels automatically annotated by formal verification tools such as Z3 and Isabelle, and empirically observes cross-task generalization from symbolic tasks to diverse reasoning benchmarks. However, this generalization phenomenon lacks any theoretical explanation, and no formal bounds exist on the generalization error, sample complexity, convergence rate, or downstream Best-of-K performance of such PRMs. We propose VeriBound, a theoretical framework that provides PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds for PRMs trained with formal verification tools. We establish four main results: (i) a PAC-Bayesian generalization bound that relates the empirical verification error on formal-verification-annotated training data to the expected error on unseen reasoning tasks, with the bound depending on the formal verification accuracy and the divergence between training and test task distributions; (ii) a sample complexity result showing that $O(d \log(d/δ) / ε^2)$ formal-verification-annotated examples suffice to achieve generalization error $ε$ with probability $1-δ$, where $d$ is the complexity of the PRM hypothesis class; (iii) a convergence analysis proving that PRM training with formal verification labels converges at a linear rate under $L$-smoothness and bounded variance conditions; and (iv) an error propagation bound that relates step-level verification error to Best-of-K performance degradation.

</details>


### [62] [From Sentiment to Actionable Insights: A Data-Driven Public Sentiment Analysis of Advanced Air Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20751)
*Esrat Farhana Dulia,Amina Dhaher,Raiful Hasan,Syed Arbab Mohd Shihab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用AI模型分析30万条社交媒体文本，评估7种情感分析模型预测公众对AAM的情绪，发现ModernBERT效果最佳，并通过LDA主题建模识别了20个主题和6大公众关注集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进空中交通的成功部署不仅依赖技术进步，更需要公众接受。理解公众情绪对识别社会障碍和制定采用策略至关重要，而现有多种情感分析模型需要评估其准确性。

Method: 收集306,009条Reddit和Quora文本，评估7种情感分析模型（基于词典、机器学习、深度学习、Transformer等方法），选择性能最佳的ModernBERT标注完整数据集，然后应用LDA进行主题建模，分析2008-2025年的时间演变和交叉情感主题。

Result: ModernBERT在AAM特定情感分类中表现最佳；识别出20个潜在主题，包括6个主要公众关注集群：劳动力和技能发展(25.29%)、法规与合规(24.64%)、无人机技术性能(20.99%)、军事、地缘政治与国防(14.58%)、安全和操作风险(8.51%)、噪音与干扰(5.98%)。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本研究提供了解决这些公众关注的可操作策略，从而改善公众接受度并支持AAM部署。研究强调了准确情感分析模型对可靠公众意见分析的重要性，并为AAM采用策略提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Advanced Air Mobility (AAM) is an emerging low-altitude air transportation system whose successful deployment depends not only on technological advancement but also on public acceptance. This acceptance will drive government support, regulations, noise standards, and willingness to fly, and in turn the overall commercial viability of AAM. Understanding public sentiment toward AAM is therefore essential for identifying its societal barriers and informing its adoption strategies. This study analyzes 306,009 human-generated texts collected from Reddit and Quora to examine public discourse on AAM using AI-based models. Because multiple sentiment analysis models exist, identifying the most accurate model is critical for reliable AAM sentiment prediction and trustworthy public opinion analysis. Accordingly, seven models spanning lexicon-based, machine learning, deep learning, and transformer-based approaches are evaluated for AAM-specific sentiment classification. ModernBERT achieves the best classification performance and is used to label the full dataset. Using the resulting sentiment labels, Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) is applied within each sentiment class to uncover latent topics in public opinion. The analysis identifies 20 distinct topics and traces their temporal evolution from 2008 to 2025. A cross-sentiment topic analysis further reveals six major clusters of public concern: workforce and skill development (25.29% of the dataset), regulation and compliance (24.64%), technical performance of drones (20.99%), military, geopolitics, and defense (14.58%), safety and operational risks (8.51%), and noise and disturbance (5.98%). Based on these findings, this study provides actionable strategies to address these concerns, thereby, improving public acceptance and support AAM deployment.

</details>


### [63] [FirstPass: Grounding AI Scientific Judgment in Multi-Round Editorial Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20769)
*Prabhjot Singh,Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FirstPass是一个AI同行评审系统，通过多轮对话数据集和响应损失掩码技术，在预测编辑决策和生成评审方面超越现有基准，实现跨学科的高效科学评审。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI同行评审系统存在三大缺陷：1)仅基于计算机科学和机器学习会议数据训练；2)忽视验证科学的迭代对话过程；3)评估侧重于风格模仿而非实际编辑判断。这些限制使得AI无法真正模拟科学评审的本质。

Method: 收集了Nature Communications的3,668个完整多轮同行评审对话，覆盖五个科学领域；采用LoRA微调Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型，进行三个任务：评审生成、评审更新、修订周期预测；核心创新是响应损失掩码技术的应用。

Result: 响应损失掩码使编辑结果预测准确率从62.0%提升至80.5%，F1-macro达78.2%，显著超越所有基准模型；生成的评审平均1,187词，接近人类参考的2,155词，ROUGE-L达0.154；在五个学科领域均表现一致。

Conclusion: FirstPass成功解决了现有AI同行评审系统的三大缺陷，通过创新的响应损失掩码技术和多领域数据集，实现了接近人类专家水平的评审能力，可作为预提交阶段的科学协作伙伴提供专业判断。

Abstract: AI systems for peer review fail on three fronts: they train on Computer Science and Machine Learning venues alone, ignore the iterative dialogue that validates science, and evaluate on stylistic mimicry rather than real editorial judgment. We introduce FirstPass, a dataset and fine-tuned model that addresses all three. Curating 3,668 complete multi-round peer-review dialogues from Nature Communications across five scientific domains (biology, chemistry, neuroscience, physics, and earth science), we exploit mandatory transparent peer review (instituted November 2022) and verify 100% content integrity by automated audit. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on three tasks: review generation, reviewer updating, and revision-cycle prediction. Our key finding is that response-only loss masking is a prerequisite, not an optimization: without it, accuracy is 62.0%, below the majority baseline; with it, FirstPass achieves 80.5% accuracy and F1-macro 78.2% on predicting editorial outcomes (Standard vs. Extended revision cycles), outperforming Gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview zero-shot by 10.4 percentage points and all baselines with statistical significance (McNemar p < 0.001). On generation, FirstPass produces reviews averaging 1,187 words, substantially closer to human references (2,155 words) than any baseline, achieving ROUGE-L 0.154 with significant gains over Qwen and DeepSeek zero-shot (p < 0.001). Deployed in the pre-submission loop as an anticipatory scientific co-author, FirstPass simulates expert critique and predicts revision cycle outcomes before submission, giving authors the judgment a trusted colleague would provide, with consistent cross-domain performance across five disciplines.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond 'One Language, One Script': Quantifying Orthographic Bias in Multilingual VLMs with PuMVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20770)
*Prabhjot Singh,Bhushan Pawar,Madhu Reddiboina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文引入了首个专门用于量化视觉语言模型中脚本依赖偏见的基准测试PuMVR，专注于旁遮普语的三种文字系统，揭示了现有模型在多脚本语言处理中的显著偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型的多语言能力存在根本性缺陷：它们假设一种语言对应单一文字系统，忽略了数十亿使用多脚本语言（如旁遮普语、塞尔维亚语、印地-乌尔都语等）的用户。这种忽视导致了文字系统偏见，可能使模型能力在不同文字变体间出现断裂。

Method: 创建了PuMVR基准测试，包含375个基于旁遮普语文化的图像推理任务，覆盖旁遮普语的三种活跃文字系统（Gurmukhi、Shahmukhi、Roman）。评估了10个最先进的视觉语言模型，分析了不同文字系统下的性能差异。

Result: 发现了显著的"脚本鸿沟"：模型经常能解决一种文字系统中的视觉推理任务，却在相同任务的另一种文字系统中失败，准确率差异高达16%，脚本一致性率低至24.8%。视觉输入虽然提升了绝对性能，但并未消除这一鸿沟，相对偏见仍然存在。推理模式在不同文字系统间转移性有限，思维链路径仅因文字系统不同而发散。

Conclusion: 提出脚本一致性率作为评估模型文字系统无关性的核心指标，挑战当前多语言评估范式，为实现更公平的人工智能提供了框架。这揭示了现有视觉语言模型在处理多脚本语言时的系统性偏见，需要重新思考多语言能力的评估方法。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are celebrated for their multilingual capabilities, yet they operate under a flawed assumption: that one language corresponds to a single writing system. This overlooks billions of users of multi-script languages like Punjabi, Serbian, Hindi-Urdu, Kurdish, among many others, for whom a model's capability may be fractured by orthographic bias. We introduce PuMVR (Punjabi Multimodal Visual Reasoning), the first benchmark designed to quantify script-dependent bias through 375 culturally grounded image-reasoning tasks across Punjabi's three active scripts (Gurmukhi, Shahmukhi, Roman). Evaluating 10 state-of-the-art VLMs, we expose a substantial Script Gap: models frequently solve visual puzzles in one script while failing identical tasks in another, with accuracy deltas reaching 16% and Script Consistency Rates (SCR) as low as 24.8%. Crucially, visual input boosts absolute performance but does not close this gap, the relative bias persists. Our analysis suggests reasoning patterns show limited cross-script transferability, and Chain-of-Thought pathways diverge based on script alone. We propose SCR as a core metric for script-agnostic evaluation, challenging current multilingual assessment paradigms and providing a framework for equitable AI.

</details>


### [65] [SciLens: Multi-modal Scientific Claim Verification with Agentic Entailment and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20873)
*Yueming Wang,Tianshi Zheng,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciLens是一个用于多模态科学声明验证的证据条件原子蕴涵框架，通过将声明分解为原子元素并与表格和图表中的具体证据进行对齐，实现结构化、可解释的验证过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学声明验证面临挑战：科学声明通常包含数值结果、比较、范围限定和解释性上下文等复杂结构，而证据则以具有不同基础结构的表格和图表形式编码。直接使用视觉语言模型进行二元判断无法有效处理这种复杂性，需要更结构化的验证方法。

Method: SciLens将科学声明分解为中心经验原子和背景原子，将中心原子与模态特定的证据证言进行对齐：对于表格，原子对齐到行、列、单元格、算术关系和表格范围；对于图表，通过面板、坐标轴、图例、视觉编码、类别、趋势、排名和限定符检查进行对齐。使用原子级蕴涵规则预测最终标签，确保只有在所有中心经验原子都被当前证据蕴涵时声明才被支持。

Result: 在SciClaimEval开发集上，SciLens达到了79.2%的宏平均F1分数和63.1%的对准确率，表明结构化智能验证既能提高证据敏感性又能改善可解释性。

Conclusion: SciLens框架通过将声明分解为原子元素并与多模态证据进行精细对齐，为科学声明验证提供了一种更精确、更可解释的方法，能够处理科学文献中常见的复杂声明结构，代表了从简单的二元判断向结构化、以证据为中心的验证方法的进步。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly relies on automated systems that generate hypotheses, inspect multimodal evidence, and validate claims at scale. Yet scientific claim verification is not well served by asking a vision-language model for a direct binary judgment: claims often combine numerical results, comparisons, scope qualifiers, and explanatory context, while evidence is encoded in tables and figures with distinct grounding structures. We present SciLens, an evidence-conditioned atomic entailment framework for multimodal scientific claim verification. SciLens decomposes each claim into central empirical atoms and background atoms, grounds the central atoms to modality-specific evidence witnesses, and predicts the final label with an atom-level entailment rule. For tables, atoms are grounded to rows, columns, cells, arithmetic relations, and table scope; for figures, they are grounded through panels, axes, legends, visual encodings, categories, trends, ranks, and qualifier checks. This yields a unified validation procedure in which a claim is supported only if every central empirical atom is entailed by the current evidence. On the SciClaimEval development set, SciLens achieves 79.2% macro-F1 and 63.1% pair accuracy, showing that structured agentic validation improves both evidence sensitivity and interpretability.

</details>


### [66] [Topic-to-Timestamp Alignment by Constrained Evidence Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20890)
*Zeynep Yılbırt,Marina Litvak,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新颖的话题-时间戳对齐方法，通过将时间戳预测重构为受限时间候选选择，改进了会议记录中话题的时间定位性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在搜索会议记录时经常记得讨论内容但忘记具体时间，传统RAG方法虽然能检索相关片段，但依赖语言模型生成时间戳，容易产生不支持或无效的时间码

Method: 将时间戳预测重构为受限时间候选选择：系统先检索带时间戳的转录片段，然后让模型选择最能支撑该话题的候选片段，而不是直接生成时间码

Result: 在420个来自200个市政会议记录的查询中，Recall@5从31.9%提升到50.0%，MAE从837.0秒减少到761.0秒，可解析输出从373个增加到419个（共420个查询）

Conclusion: 长转录本中的时间定位性能高度依赖于检索质量和输出设计，而不仅仅是语言模型的选择，将时间戳生成转化为候选选择显著提升了准确性和可靠性

Abstract: Meeting archives are difficult to search when users remember what was discussed but not when. We study topic-to-timestamp alignment: given a natural-language topic and a timestamped meeting transcript, the goal is to return the time at which the topic is discussed. A standard RAG setup can retrieve relevant transcript excerpts, but still asks the language model to generate a timestamp, which can produce unsupported or invalid timecodes. We therefore recast timestamp prediction as constrained temporal candidate selection: the system retrieves timestamped transcript chunks, and the model selects the candidate that best grounds the topic instead of generating a timecode. On 420 topic-timestamp queries from 200 municipal meeting transcripts, this increases Recall@5 from 31.9% to 50.0%, reduces MAE from 837.0 seconds to 761.0 seconds with Mistral-7B-Instruct, and increases the number of parseable outputs from 373 to 419 of 420 queries. The results suggest that temporal grounding in long transcripts depends strongly on retrieval quality and output design, not only on the choice of the language model.

</details>


### [67] [PeerCheck: Enhancing LLM-Generated Academic Reviews Towards Human-Level Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20897)
*Zeyuan Chen,Ziqing Yang,Yihan Ma,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PeerCheck框架，研究LLM生成评审与人工评审的差异，并通过提示工程和RAG技术提高LLM评审质量，发现CoT显著改善质量但RAG效果不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术投稿数量增长使传统同行评审难以应对，出现使用大语言模型协助评审的趋势，但需要提高LLM生成评审的质量以确保评审系统的公正性和有效性。

Method: 1. 提出PeerCheck框架；2. 分析LLM与人类评审差异；3. 采用提示工程（如Chain-of-Thought）和检索增强生成技术提升LLM评审质量。

Result: 发现LLM与人类关注点不同（LLM重理论，人类重方法和实验）；CoT显著提高LLM评审质量；发现"RAG悖论"-RAG对不同LLM效果不一，某些情况下甚至降低评审质量。

Conclusion: LLM生成学术评审既有潜力也有局限，CoT是有效方法但RAG需谨慎使用，研究为构建更有效、与人类对齐的评审系统提供见解，相关数据集已开源。

Abstract: As academic submissions grow, the traditional peer review process struggles to keep up, raising concerns about quality and fairness. A trend of using large language models (LLMs) for assistance has emerged. In this work, we take a critical step toward improving the quality of LLM-generated reviews. We propose the PeerCheck framework, which investigates LLM-human review differences (RQ1) and explores methods to improve LLM-generated review quality (RQ2). We first analyzed the human-written reviews with reviews generated by various LLMs and found that LLMs and humans focus on different terms, e.g., LLMs prioritize theory while humans emphasize methodology and experiments. We further adopt prompt engineering, such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and utilize retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to enhance the LLM-generated reviews towards human-level quality. We find CoT significantly improves the quality of LLM reviews, while we discover an unexpected "RAG paradox," i.e., experiments with RAG produce different results for various LLMs and, in some cases, even reduce review quality. Our comprehensive analysis of LLM-generated academic reviews illustrates both possibilities and limitations, contributing to a more effective, human-aligned review system. Our dataset is available on https://github.com/TrustAIRLab/PeerCheck.

</details>


### [68] [Storyline Trees: Hierarchical Representations for Long-Form Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20900)
*Litu Ou,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了故事线树结构，通过分层表示长篇叙事，实现了自适应检索，在长篇叙事问答任务中超越了现有基线模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇叙事对长上下文模型具有挑战性，因为其结构是隐含的：事件、角色和情节在数百页中相互作用，缺乏结构化文档中的显式导航线索

Method: 1. 将章节分割为连续的叙事片段（场景）作为基本构建单元
2. 通过互补的自顶向下和自底向上过程推断故事线树：推导、细化、聚类和总结多层次抽象的故事线
3. 实现自适应检索：让模型迭代检查高层叙事结构，按需检索场景级证据

Result: 在三个长上下文叙事问答基准测试中，自适应检索方法优于强基线，包括后训练的长上下文模型和基于分块的智能体方法。消融实验确认：1) 场景比章节或通用分割更有效；2) 在匹配检索预算下增益依然存在

Conclusion: 故事线树为长叙事提供了一种有效的分层表示，通过自适应检索机制显著提升了长上下文模型的问答性能，证明了结构化表示在理解隐含叙事结构中的重要性

Abstract: Long-form narratives are challenging for long-context models because their structure is implicit: events, characters, and plotlines interact across hundreds of pages without the explicit cues that guide navigation in structured documents. We address this by constructing storyline trees, hierarchical representations that organize narratives from global themes and major plotlines to fine-grained events. We first segment chapters into contiguous narrative segments, or scenes, and use them as the basic units for tree construction. We then infer storyline trees through complementary top-down and bottom-up procedures that derive, refine, cluster, and summarize storylines at multiple levels of abstraction. We showcase the utility of this representation for question answering: storyline trees enable adaptive retrieval, allowing models to iteratively inspect high-level narrative structure and retrieve scene-level evidence on demand. Experiments on three long-context narrative QA benchmarks show that adaptive retrieval outperforms strong baselines, including post-trained long-context models and agentic chunk-based methods. Ablations confirm that scenes are more effective basic units than chapters or generic segmentation, and that gains persist under matched retrieval budgets

</details>


### [69] [Latent Personal Memory: Represent personal memory as dynamic soft prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20911)
*Debrup Das,Avinash Amballa,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LPM是一个用于个性化LLM的框架，通过将用户特定历史表示为紧凑的潜在槽矩阵，大幅减少KV缓存使用和可训练参数，同时保持或超越现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化LLM方法在处理长期用户行为模式时面临计算效率低、可扩展性差以及与冻结基础模型兼容性等问题，需要一种更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出Latent Personal Memory (LPM)框架，将用户特定历史编码为紧凑的N槽潜在矩阵，通过共享的交叉注意力投影网络将这些槽映射为动态的、输入条件化的软提示，预置到冻结LLM的输入前。

Result: 在PersonaMem v1基准上，LPM比LoRA和Prompt Tuning分别高出8.8%和54.4%的总体准确率，同时减少KV缓存使用64倍以上；在LoCOMO基准上，以少120倍的可训练参数匹配LoRA准确率；在128K上下文长度下超越全上下文方法。

Conclusion: LPM提供了一个可扩展、高效且与冻结基础模型兼容的个性化LLM框架，通过紧凑的潜在记忆表示实现了优异的性能表现和计算效率，特别适合处理长上下文场景。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires encoding long-term, user-specific behavioral patterns in a way that is computationally efficient, scalable, and compatible with a frozen base model. We present Latent Personal Memory (LPM), a scalable framework that represents user-specific history as a compact, persistent matrix of N latent slots, that are interpretable. A shared cross-attention projection network maps these slots into dynamic, input-conditioned soft prompts that are prepended to the input of a frozen LLM. We evaluate LPM on PersonaMem v1 and LoCOMO benchmarks across Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B backbones. Results demonstrate that LPM outperforms LoRA and Prompt Tuning by up to 8.8% and 54.4% in overall accuracy respectively on PersonaMem v1, while reducing KV-cache usage by over 64x. On LoCoMo, LPM matches LoRA accuracy with 120x fewer trainable parameters. We also show that the efficiency of LPM grows with context length and outperforms full-context at 128K context length.

</details>
